# Second Conflict

Journal: `quests/side_quest/sq011_johnny` · `SideQuest` · hash `3025872767`.

Journal premise: Ah, just like old times. Never knew until we stepped onstage whether we'd have a full crew or not, whether Henry was too high outta his mind or whether Denny was locked up again. Most important thing is to find Nancy – she'll take care of everything. Always did, always will. And you wanna see Samurai on stage, don't you?

**Coverage: journal-linked.** 163 exact journal-linked nodes in 14 resources; 26 resources in the inspected dependency context.

Resource roles in that context: 24 content candidate, 2 repair. Role labels describe path/name evidence, not proof of runtime reachability.

Root quest resources with additional journal references: none indexed. Roots remain activation context when a phase/scene anchor exists, to avoid importing sibling launch scaffolding.

World NodeRef definitions/streaming owners, full actor and animation closure, numeric TweakDB names, spoken subtitle/VO payload joins and installed/save behavior have not been validated by this structural pass. Raw direct references, actor bindings, choices, event locstrings and community entries remain in the evidence shards.

## Required acceptance cases before reuse

Exercise normal completion and each named alternate outcome; interrupt and return at every scene handoff; start with spatial/item/actor predicates already satisfied; save/load across waits and joins; cancel parallel listeners; verify reward delivery once; and verify cleanup without changing the committed outcome. Apply the block-specific cases in the [expanded contracts](../all-quest-blocks.md). No installed playtest is claimed.

## Connected paths and supporting bindings

2389 repeated typed/socket path signatures in this selected graph context; 364 distinct direct resource identities; 42 embedded graph handoffs. The full occurrence lists, concrete parameters, exact source pointers and external edges are retained in the checked evidence. Shared forward dependencies can serve several quests; these are context counts.
